FILE: frontend/src/app/analytics/page.tsx
================================================
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState, useMemo } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import AppBar from "@mui/material/AppBar";
import Toolbar from "@mui/material/Toolbar";
import Typography from "@mui/material/Typography";
import IconButton from "@mui/material/IconButton";
import TextField from "@mui/material/TextField";
import Tabs from "@mui/material/Tabs";
import Tab from "@mui/material/Tab";
import Box from "@mui/material/Box";
import Alert from "@mui/material/Alert";
import CircularProgress from "@mui/material/CircularProgress";
import { DataGrid } from "@mui/x-data-grid";
import type { GridColDef, GridRowSelectionModel } from "@mui/x-data-grid";
import {
  ComposedChart,
  Line,
  Area,
  BarChart,
  Bar,
  ReferenceLine,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
  CartesianGrid,
  Tooltip,
  Legend,
  ResponsiveContainer,
} from "recharts";
import type {
  MasterIndex,
  NodeEntry,
  PITBin,
} from "@/types/payload";

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Types
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

interface NodeRow {
  id: number;
  location_name: string;
  is_simulated: boolean;
  avg_mu: number;
  max_mu: number;
  avg_sigma: number;
  semantic_tags: string;
  mae_drop_nan: number | null;
  rmse_drop_nan: number | null;
  mae_fill_nan: number | null;
  rmse_fill_nan: number | null;
}

// Each chart point has numeric keys for mu and ci bounds per selected node.
type ChartPoint = Record<string, number>;

// Diagonal data uses the same axis keys as the reliability chart (pi_center / observed_zero_fraction)
// so that the Line component can resolve them against the configured axes.
const RELIABILITY_DIAGONAL = [
  { pi_center: 0, observed_zero_fraction: 0 },
  { pi_center: 1, observed_zero_fraction: 1 },
];

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Colour palette for multi-node overlay
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

const PALETTE = [
  "#60a5fa", // blue-400
  "#34d399", // emerald-400
  "#f59e0b", // amber-400
  "#f87171", // red-400
  "#a78bfa", // violet-400
  "#fb7185", // rose-400
  "#22d3ee", // cyan-400
  "#4ade80", // green-400
];

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Main component
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

export default function AnalyticsPage() {
  const [masterIndex, setMasterIndex] = useState<MasterIndex | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [filterText, setFilterText] = useState("");
  const [selectedIds, setSelectedIds] = useState<number[]>([]);
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch("/data/master_index.json")
      .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : null))
      .then((d: MasterIndex | null) => {
        setMasterIndex(d);
        setLoading(false);
      })
      .catch(() => setLoading(false));
  }, []);

  // ---- DataGrid rows (computed aggregates) --------------------------------

  const rows: NodeRow[] = useMemo(() => {
    if (!masterIndex) return [];
    return masterIndex.nodes.map((node: NodeEntry) => {
      const preds = node.timeseries.map((e) => e.prediction);
      const sigmas = node.timeseries.map((e) => e.uncertainty);
      const n = preds.length || 1;
      const avg_mu = preds.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / n;
      const max_mu = preds.length ? Math.max(...preds) : 0;
      const avg_sigma = sigmas.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / n;
      const te = node.two_extremes;
      return {
        id: node.id,
        location_name: node.location_name,
        is_simulated: node.is_simulated,
        avg_mu,
        max_mu,
        avg_sigma,
        semantic_tags: node.semantic_tags?.join(", ") || "—",
        mae_drop_nan: te?.mae_drop_nan ?? null,
        rmse_drop_nan: te?.rmse_drop_nan ?? null,
        mae_fill_nan: te?.mae_fill_nan ?? null,
        rmse_fill_nan: te?.rmse_fill_nan ?? null,
      };
    });
  }, [masterIndex]);

  // ---- Omnibar filter -----------------------------------------------------

  const filteredRows = useMemo(() => {
    const q = filterText.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!q) return rows;
    return rows.filter(
      (r) =>
        r.location_name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        String(r.id) === filterText.trim() ||
        r.semantic_tags.toLowerCase().includes(q)
    );
  }, [rows, filterText]);

  // ---- Chart data (Amendment 1: separate ci_lower / ci_upper fields) -------

  const chartData: ChartPoint[] = useMemo(() => {
    if (!masterIndex || selectedIds.length === 0) return [];
    const nodeMap = new Map(masterIndex.nodes.map((n) => [n.id, n]));
    const T = masterIndex.metadata.timesteps;
    return Array.from({ length: T }, (_, t) => {
      const point: ChartPoint = { t };
      for (const id of selectedIds) {
        const node = nodeMap.get(id);
        if (!node) continue;
        const ts = node.timeseries[t];
        if (!ts) continue;
        const halfCI = 1.96 * ts.uncertainty;
        point[`mu_${id}`] = ts.prediction;
        point[`ci_lower_${id}`] = Math.max(0, ts.prediction - halfCI);
        point[`ci_upper_${id}`] = ts.prediction + halfCI;
      }
      return point;
    });
  }, [masterIndex, selectedIds]);

  // ---- Reliability chart data (dots + diagonal in one ComposedChart) -------
  // The diagonal Line uses RELIABILITY_DIAGONAL with its own `data` prop.
  // The scatter Line uses the reliability bins from the payload.

  const reliabilityData = useMemo(
    () => masterIndex?.zero_inflation_reliability ?? [],
    [masterIndex]
  );

  // ---- DataGrid column definitions ----------------------------------------

  const nullableFmt = (value: unknown): string => {
    if (value === null || value === undefined) return "—";
    return (value as number).toFixed(3);
  };

  const columns: GridColDef<NodeRow>[] = [
    { field: "id", headerName: "ID", width: 64, type: "number" },
    { field: "location_name", headerName: "Location", flex: 1, minWidth: 150 },
    { field: "is_simulated", headerName: "Sim", width: 56, type: "boolean" },
    {
      field: "avg_mu",
      headerName: "Avg μ",
      width: 95,
      type: "number",
      valueFormatter: (value: number) => value.toFixed(3),
    },
    {
      field: "max_mu",
      headerName: "Peak μ",
      width: 95,
      type: "number",
      valueFormatter: (value: number) => value.toFixed(3),
    },
    {
      field: "avg_sigma",
      headerName: "Avg σ",
      width: 95,
      type: "number",
      valueFormatter: (value: number) => value.toFixed(3),
    },
    {
      field: "mae_drop_nan",
      headerName: "MAE (drop NaN)",
      width: 130,
      type: "number",
      valueFormatter: nullableFmt,
    },
    {
      field: "rmse_drop_nan",
      headerName: "RMSE (drop NaN)",
      width: 140,
      type: "number",
      valueFormatter: nullableFmt,
    },
    {
      field: "mae_fill_nan",
      headerName: "MAE (fill 0)",
      width: 110,
      type: "number",
      valueFormatter: nullableFmt,
    },
    {
      field: "rmse_fill_nan",
      headerName: "RMSE (fill 0)",
      width: 115,
      type: "number",
      valueFormatter: nullableFmt,
    },
    {
      field: "semantic_tags",
      headerName: "POI Tags",
      flex: 1,
      minWidth: 120,
      sortable: false,
    },
  ];

  // ---- Loading / error states ---------------------------------------------

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <Box
        sx={{
          display: "flex",
          height: "100vh",
          alignItems: "center",
          justifyContent: "center",
          bgcolor: "#0a0a0a",
        }}
      >
        <CircularProgress sx={{ color: "#60a5fa" }} />
      </Box>
    );
  }

  if (!masterIndex) {
    return (
      <Box
        sx={{
          display: "flex",
          height: "100vh",
          alignItems: "center",
          justifyContent: "center",
          bgcolor: "#0a0a0a",
        }}
      >
        <Alert severity="error">
          Failed to load master_index.json — check that{" "}
          <code>export_ui_payload.py</code> has been run.
        </Alert>
      </Box>
    );
  }

  const hasPIT =
    Array.isArray(masterIndex.pit_histogram) &&
    masterIndex.pit_histogram.length > 0;
  const hasReliability = reliabilityData.length > 0;

  // ---- Full layout --------------------------------------------------------

  return (
    <Box
      sx={{
        display: "flex",
        flexDirection: "column",
        height: "100vh",
        bgcolor: "#0a0a0a",
        color: "#e2e8f0",
        overflow: "hidden",
      }}
    >
      {/* AppBar */}
      <AppBar
        position="static"
        elevation={0}
        sx={{
          bgcolor: "rgba(12,12,12,0.97)",
          borderBottom: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.07)",
        }}
      >
        <Toolbar variant="dense" sx={{ minHeight: 48 }}>
          <Link href="/" style={{ color: "inherit", display: "flex" }}>
            <IconButton
              size="small"
              sx={{ color: "#60a5fa", mr: 1 }}
              aria-label="Back to map"
            >
              <svg
                width="20"
                height="20"
                viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                fill="currentColor"
              >
                <path d="M20 11H7.83l5.59-5.59L12 4l-8 8 8 8 1.41-1.41L7.83 13H20v-2z" />
              </svg>
            </IconButton>
          </Link>

          <Typography
            variant="subtitle1"
            sx={{ fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: "0.05em", color: "#e2e8f0" }}
          >
            NSTAR Analytics
          </Typography>

          <Typography
            variant="caption"
            sx={{ ml: 2, color: "#334155", fontFamily: "monospace" }}
          >
            {masterIndex.metadata.num_nodes} nodes ·{" "}
            {masterIndex.metadata.timesteps}T ·{" "}
            {masterIndex.metadata.start_date}
          </Typography>
        </Toolbar>
      </AppBar>

      <Box
        sx={{
          flex: 1,
          display: "flex",
          flexDirection: "column",
          overflow: "hidden",
          p: 1.5,
          gap: 1.5,
        }}
      >
        {/* Omnibar */}
        <TextField
          fullWidth
          size="small"
          placeholder="Filter by location name, node ID, or POI tag…"
          value={filterText}
          onChange={(e) => setFilterText(e.target.value)}
          inputProps={{ "aria-label": "Filter nodes" }}
          sx={{
            "& .MuiOutlinedInput-root": {
              bgcolor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.03)",
              "& fieldset": { borderColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.09)" },
              "&:hover fieldset": { borderColor: "rgba(96,165,250,0.35)" },
              "&.Mui-focused fieldset": { borderColor: "#60a5fa" },
            },
            "& input": { color: "#e2e8f0", fontSize: "0.85rem" },
            "& input::placeholder": { color: "#334155", opacity: 1 },
          }}
        />

        {/* DataGrid */}
        <Box sx={{ height: "36vh", minHeight: 160 }}>
          <DataGrid
            rows={filteredRows}
            columns={columns}
            checkboxSelection
            disableRowSelectionOnClick
            rowSelectionModel={selectedIds as GridRowSelectionModel}
            onRowSelectionModelChange={(ids: GridRowSelectionModel) =>
              setSelectedIds(ids as number[])
            }
            initialState={{
              sorting: { sortModel: [{ field: "avg_mu", sort: "desc" }] },
              pagination: { paginationModel: { pageSize: 25 } },
            }}
            pageSizeOptions={[25, 50, 100]}
            density="compact"
            sx={{
              border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.06)",
              bgcolor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.01)",
              color: "#cbd5e1",
              fontSize: "0.78rem",
              "& .MuiDataGrid-columnHeaders": {
                bgcolor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.03)",
                borderBottom: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.06)",
              },
              "& .MuiDataGrid-columnHeaderTitle": {
                color: "#64748b",
                fontWeight: 600,
                fontSize: "0.75rem",
              },
              "& .MuiDataGrid-cell": {
                borderBottom: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.03)",
              },
              "& .MuiDataGrid-row:hover": {
                bgcolor: "rgba(96,165,250,0.05)",
              },
              "& .MuiDataGrid-row.Mui-selected": {
                bgcolor: "rgba(96,165,250,0.09)",
              },
              "& .MuiDataGrid-row.Mui-selected:hover": {
                bgcolor: "rgba(96,165,250,0.13)",
              },
              "& .MuiCheckbox-root": { color: "#334155" },
              "& .MuiCheckbox-root.Mui-checked": { color: "#60a5fa" },
              "& .MuiDataGrid-footerContainer": {
                borderTop: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.06)",
                color: "#334155",
              },
              "& .MuiTablePagination-root": {
                color: "#334155",
                fontSize: "0.75rem",
              },
              "& .MuiDataGrid-virtualScroller": { bgcolor: "transparent" },
            }}
          />
        </Box>

        {/* Tabs */}
        <Box sx={{ borderBottom: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.06)" }}>
          <Tabs
            value={activeTab}
            onChange={(_, v: number) => setActiveTab(v)}
            sx={{
              minHeight: 34,
              "& .MuiTab-root": {
                color: "#334155",
                textTransform: "none",
                fontSize: "0.78rem",
                minHeight: 34,
                py: 0,
                px: 2,
              },
              "& .Mui-selected": { color: "#60a5fa !important" },
              "& .Mui-disabled": { color: "#1e293b !important", opacity: 1 },
              "& .MuiTabs-indicator": { bgcolor: "#60a5fa", height: 2 },
            }}
          >
            <Tab label="Forecast Variance" value={0} />
            <Tab label="Network Influence" value={1} />
            <Tab label="Self-Healing Log" value={2} />
            <Tab label="ZINB Calibration" value={3} />
          </Tabs>
        </Box>

        {/* Tab 0 — Forecast Variance */}
        {activeTab === 0 && (
          <Box sx={{ flex: 1, overflow: "hidden", minHeight: 0 }}>
            {selectedIds.length === 0 ? (
              <Alert
                severity="info"
                sx={{
                  bgcolor: "rgba(96,165,250,0.07)",
                  color: "#93c5fd",
                  border: "1px solid rgba(96,165,250,0.15)",
                  "& .MuiAlert-icon": { color: "#60a5fa" },
                  fontSize: "0.82rem",
                }}
              >
                Select one or more nodes from the table above to plot their
                ZINB forecast variance (μ ± 1.96σ confidence interval).
              </Alert>
            ) : (
              <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                <ComposedChart
                  data={chartData}
                  margin={{ top: 4, right: 20, left: -12, bottom: 24 }}
                >
                  <CartesianGrid
                    strokeDasharray="3 3"
                    stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.04)"
                  />
                  <XAxis
                    dataKey="t"
                    stroke="#1e293b"
                    tick={{ fill: "#334155", fontSize: 10 }}
                    label={{
                      value: "Timestep (15-min intervals)",
                      position: "insideBottom",
                      offset: -12,
                      fill: "#334155",
                      fontSize: 10,
                    }}
                  />
                  <YAxis
                    stroke="#1e293b"
                    tick={{ fill: "#334155", fontSize: 10 }}
                    width={48}
                  />
                  <Tooltip
                    contentStyle={{
                      background: "rgba(10,10,10,0.96)",
                      border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08)",
                      color: "#e2e8f0",
                      fontSize: "0.72rem",
                      borderRadius: 6,
                    }}
                    formatter={(value: unknown, name: string) => [
                      Array.isArray(value)
                        ? `[${(value as number[]).map((v) => v.toFixed(2)).join(", ")}]`
                        : Number(value).toFixed(3),
                      name,
                    ]}
                  />
                  <Legend
                    wrapperStyle={{ color: "#475569", fontSize: "0.72rem" }}
                  />

                  {selectedIds.map((id, i) => {
                    const color = PALETTE[i % PALETTE.length];
                    const node = masterIndex.nodes.find((n) => n.id === id);
                    const label = node
                      ? node.location_name.replace(/^Ncl/, "")
                      : `Node ${id}`;

                    return [
                      /*
                       * Amendment 1 — native Recharts range Area.
                       * dataKey receives an array of two field names:
                       * Recharts reads [ci_lower_{id}, ci_upper_{id}] from
                       * each data point and fills the band between them.
                       */
                      <Area
                        key={`ci_${id}`}
                        type="monotone"
                        dataKey={
                          [
                            `ci_lower_${id}`,
                            `ci_upper_${id}`,
                          ] as unknown as string
                        }
                        fill={color}
                        stroke="none"
                        fillOpacity={0.13}
                        name={`${label} 95% CI`}
                        legendType="square"
                        activeDot={false}
                        isAnimationActive={false}
                      />,
                      <Line
                        key={`mu_${id}`}
                        type="monotone"
                        dataKey={`mu_${id}`}
                        stroke={color}
                        strokeWidth={2}
                        dot={false}
                        name={`${label} μ`}
                        isAnimationActive={false}
                      />,
                    ];
                  })}
                </ComposedChart>
              </ResponsiveContainer>
            )}
          </Box>
        )}

        {/* Tab 1 — Network Influence */}
        {activeTab === 1 && (
          <Box sx={{ flex: 1, display: "flex", gap: 2, overflow: "hidden", flexDirection: { xs: "column", md: "row" } }}>
            <Box sx={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 300, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column" }}>
              <Typography variant="subtitle2" sx={{ mb: 1, color: "#94a3b8" }}>
                Alpha-Node Leaderboard
              </Typography>
              <Box sx={{ flex: 1, minHeight: 0 }}>
                <DataGrid
                  rows={(masterIndex?.alpha_node_leaderboard || []).map((r, i) => ({ id: r.node_id, ...r }))}
                  columns={[
                    { field: "node_id", headerName: "ID", width: 60 },
                    { field: "location_name", headerName: "Location", flex: 1 },
                    { field: "total_outgoing_attention", headerName: "Outgoing Attention", width: 150, valueFormatter: (value: number) => value.toFixed(3) }
                  ]}
                  density="compact"
                  hideFooter
                  sx={{
                    bgcolor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.02)", color: "#cbd5e1",
                    "& .MuiDataGrid-columnHeaders": { bgcolor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.03)", borderBottom: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.06)" },
                    "& .MuiDataGrid-cell": { borderBottom: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.03)" },
                  }}
                />
              </Box>
            </Box>
            <Box sx={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 300 }}>
              <Typography variant="subtitle2" sx={{ mb: 1, color: "#94a3b8" }}>

              </Typography>

                <ComposedChart data={(masterIndex?.attention_entropy_series || []).map((v, i) => ({ t: i, entropy: v }))}>
                  <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.04)" />

                  <YAxis stroke="#1e293b" tick={{ fill: "#334155" }} />
                  <Tooltip contentStyle={{ background: "rgba(10,10,10,0.9)", borderColor: "#334155" }} />
                  <Line type="monotone" dataKey="entropy" stroke="#a78bfa" dot={false} strokeWidth={2} name="Shannon Entropy" />
                </ComposedChart>
              </ResponsiveContainer>
            </Box>
          </Box>
        )}

        {/* Tab 2 — Self-Healing Log */}
        {activeTab === 2 && (
          <Box sx={{ flex: 1, display: "flex", gap: 2, overflow: "hidden", flexDirection: { xs: "column", md: "row" } }}>
            <Box sx={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 300 }}>
              <Typography variant="subtitle2" sx={{ mb: 1, color: "#94a3b8" }}>
                Global Imputation Burden vs. Healing Quality
              </Typography>
              <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={300}>
                <ComposedChart data={(masterIndex?.global_imputation_burden || []).map((b, i) => ({
                    t: i,
                    burden: b * 100,
                    mse: masterIndex?.global_healing_quality?.[i] || 0
                  }))}>
                  <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.04)" />

                  <Line yAxisId="right" type="monotone" dataKey="mse" stroke="#60a5fa" dot={false} strokeWidth={2} name="Global Imputation MSE" />

              </ResponsiveContainer>
            </Box>
            <Box sx={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 300, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column" }}>
              <Typography variant="subtitle2" sx={{ mb: 1, color: "#94a3b8" }}>
                Tripwire Audit Trail
              </Typography>
              <Box sx={{ flex: 1, minHeight: 0 }}>
                <DataGrid
                  rows={(masterIndex?.tripwire_audit_trail || []).map((r, i) => ({ id: i, ...r }))}
                  columns={[
                    { field: "timestep", headerName: "Time", width: 60 },
                    { field: "node_id", headerName: "Node ID", width: 80 },

                  density="compact"
                  sx={{

                    "& .MuiDataGrid-columnHeaders": { bgcolor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.03)", borderBottom: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.06)" },
                    "& .MuiDataGrid-cell": { borderBottom: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.03)" },
                  }}
                />
              </Box>
            </Box>
          </Box>
        )}

        {/* Tab 3 — ZINB Calibration */}
        {activeTab === 3 && (
          <Box
            sx={{
              flex: 1,
              overflow: "auto",
              minHeight: 0,
              display: "flex",
              flexDirection: "column",
              gap: 2,
            }}
          >
            {!hasPIT && !hasReliability ? (
              <Alert
                severity="info"
                sx={{
                  bgcolor: "rgba(96,165,250,0.07)",
                  color: "#93c5fd",
                  border: "1px solid rgba(96,165,250,0.15)",
                  "& .MuiAlert-icon": { color: "#60a5fa" },
                  fontSize: "0.82rem",
                }}
              >
                Calibration data not present in this payload. Re-run{" "}
                <code>export_ui_payload.py</code> to generate the PIT histogram
                and zero-inflation reliability arrays.
              </Alert>
            ) : (
              <Box
                sx={{
                  display: "flex",
                  gap: 2,
                  flex: 1,
                  minHeight: 300,
                  flexWrap: "wrap",
                }}
              >
                {/* ---- Left: PIT Histogram ---- */}
                <Box
                  sx={{
                    flex: 1,

                    display: "flex",
                    flexDirection: "column",

                    variant="caption"

                    PIT Histogram — uniform bars indicate perfect calibration
                  </Typography>
                  {hasPIT ? (
                    <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={280}>
                      <BarChart

                        <CartesianGrid
                          strokeDasharray="3 3"
                          stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.04)"
                        />
                        <XAxis
                          dataKey="bin_center"
                          stroke="#1e293b"
                          tick={{ fill: "#334155", fontSize: 10 }}
                          tickFormatter={(v: number) => v.toFixed(2)}
                          label={{
                            value: "PIT value  u = F_ZINB(y_obs)",
                            position: "insideBottom",
                            offset: -14,
                            fill: "#334155",
                            fontSize: 10,
                          }}
                        />
                        <YAxis
                          stroke="#1e293b"
                          tick={{ fill: "#334155", fontSize: 10 }}
                          width={48}
                          label={{
                            value: "Density",
                            angle: -90,
                            position: "insideLeft",
                            offset: 14,
                            fill: "#334155",
                            fontSize: 10,
                          }}
                        />
                        <Tooltip
                          contentStyle={{
                            background: "rgba(10,10,10,0.96)",
                            border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08)",
                            color: "#e2e8f0",
                            fontSize: "0.72rem",
                            borderRadius: 6,
                          }}
                          formatter={(v: unknown) => [
                            Number(v).toFixed(4),
                            "density",
                          ]}
                        />
                        {/* Uniform reference: 1/10 = 0.10 */}
                        <ReferenceLine
                          y={0.1}
                          stroke="#f59e0b"
                          strokeDasharray="4 4"
                          label={{
                            value: "Uniform (0.10)",
                            position: "insideTopRight",
                            fill: "#f59e0b",
                            fontSize: 9,
                          }}
                        />
                        <Bar
                          dataKey="density"
                          fill="#60a5fa"
                          fillOpacity={0.8}
                          isAnimationActive={false}
                        />
                      </BarChart>
                    </ResponsiveContainer>
                  ) : (
                    <Alert severity="warning" sx={{ fontSize: "0.78rem" }}>
                      PIT histogram not available in this payload.
                    </Alert>
                  )}
                </Box>

                {/* ---- Right: Zero-Inflation Reliability ---- */}
                <Box
                  sx={{
                    flex: 1,
                    minWidth: 300,
                    display: "flex",
                    flexDirection: "column",
                    gap: 0.5,
                  }}
                >
                  <Typography
                    variant="caption"
                    sx={{ color: "#64748b", fontSize: "0.75rem" }}
                  >
                    Zero-Inflation Reliability — points on the dashed y = x
                    line indicate perfect calibration
                  </Typography>
                  {hasReliability ? (
                    <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={280}>
                      {/*
                       * ComposedChart with two Line series:
                       *   1. Scatter dots (stroke=none, large dots) — observed zero fractions
                       *   2. Dashed diagonal (y = x) — perfect calibration reference
                       * Both use the same axis keys (pi_center / observed_zero_fraction) so
                       * the diagonal data also uses those key names for correct axis resolution.
                       */}
                      <ComposedChart
                        margin={{ top: 8, right: 16, left: -4, bottom: 28 }}
                      >
                        <CartesianGrid
                          strokeDasharray="3 3"
                          stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.04)"
                        />
                        <XAxis
                          dataKey="pi_center"
                          type="number"
                          domain={[0, 1]}
                          stroke="#1e293b"
                          tick={{ fill: "#334155", fontSize: 10 }}
                          tickFormatter={(v: number) => v.toFixed(1)}
                          label={{
                            value: "Predicted π (zero-inflation probability)",
                            position: "insideBottom",
                            offset: -14,
                            fill: "#334155",
                            fontSize: 10,
                          }}
                        />
                        <YAxis
                          dataKey="observed_zero_fraction"
                          type="number"
                          domain={[0, 1]}
                          stroke="#1e293b"
                          tick={{ fill: "#334155", fontSize: 10 }}
                          width={48}
                          label={{
                            value: "Observed zero fraction",
                            angle: -90,
                            position: "insideLeft",
                            offset: 14,
                            fill: "#334155",
                            fontSize: 10,
                          }}
                        />
                        <Tooltip
                          contentStyle={{
                            background: "rgba(10,10,10,0.96)",
                            border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08)",
                            color: "#e2e8f0",
                            fontSize: "0.72rem",
                            borderRadius: 6,
                          }}
                          formatter={(v: unknown, name: string) => [
                            Number(v).toFixed(4),
                            name,
                          ]}
                        />
                        <Legend
                          wrapperStyle={{
                            color: "#475569",
                            fontSize: "0.72rem",
                          }}
                        />
                        {/* y = x diagonal — same axis keys, two endpoint data points */}
                        <Line
                          data={RELIABILITY_DIAGONAL}
                          type="linear"
                          dataKey="observed_zero_fraction"
                          stroke="#f59e0b"
                          strokeDasharray="6 3"
                          strokeWidth={1.5}
                          dot={false}
                          activeDot={false}
                          name="Perfect calibration (y = x)"
                          isAnimationActive={false}
                          legendType="plainline"
                        />
                        {/* Observed scatter — dots only, no connecting line */}
                        <Line
                          data={reliabilityData}
                          type="monotone"
                          dataKey="observed_zero_fraction"
                          stroke="none"
                          dot={{ r: 5, fill: "#34d399", stroke: "#34d399" }}
                          activeDot={{ r: 7, fill: "#34d399" }}
                          name="Observed zero fraction"
                          isAnimationActive={false}
                          legendType="circle"
                        />
                      </ComposedChart>
                    </ResponsiveContainer>
                  ) : (
                    <Alert severity="warning" sx={{ fontSize: "0.78rem" }}>
                      Reliability diagram not available in this payload.
                    </Alert>
                  )}
                </Box>
              </Box>
            )}
          </Box>
        )}
      </Box>
    </Box>
  );

FILE: frontend/src/app/api/log/route.ts
================================================
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';

// process.cwd() returns /workspace/frontend inside the container (the mounted
// host volume), so the log persists on the host at ./frontend/frontend_runtime_errors.log
const LOG_PATH = path.join(process.cwd(), 'frontend_runtime_errors.log');

export async function POST(request: NextRequest): Promise<NextResponse> {
  try {
    const body = (await request.json()) as Record<string, unknown>;
    const entry = JSON.stringify({ timestamp: new Date().toISOString(), ...body }) + '\n';
    fs.appendFileSync(LOG_PATH, entry);
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, path: LOG_PATH }, { status: 200 });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('[/api/log] Write failed:', err);
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: String(err) }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function DELETE(): Promise<NextResponse> {
  try {
    fs.writeFileSync(LOG_PATH, '');
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, truncated: true }, { status: 200 });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('[/api/log] Truncate failed:', err);
    return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: String(err) }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

FILE: frontend/src/components/AttentionControls.tsx
================================================
'use client';

import useTimeStore from '@/store/useTimeStore';

/**
 * Floating global attention-threshold control.
 *
 * Positioned top-left below the Analytics Fab. Controls the minimum edge
 * weight required for a global topology arc to be rendered. Hidden when a
 * node is selected because the global topology layer is hidden in that state
 * (the ego-graph layer takes over and ignores the threshold).
 */
export default function AttentionControls() {
  const selectedNodeId = useTimeStore((s) => s.selectedNodeId);
  const globalAttentionThreshold = useTimeStore((s) => s.globalAttentionThreshold);
  const setGlobalAttentionThreshold = useTimeStore((s) => s.setGlobalAttentionThreshold);

  if (selectedNodeId !== null) return null;

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        position: 'absolute',
        top: 88,
        left: 16,
        zIndex: 20,
        minWidth: 180,
        background: 'rgba(15,15,15,0.88)',
        border: '1px solid rgba(96,165,250,0.25)',
        borderRadius: 10,
        backdropFilter: 'blur(8px)',
        padding: '10px 14px',
        boxShadow: '0 4px 24px rgba(0,0,0,0.5)',
      }}
      aria-label="Global attention threshold controls"
    >
      <label
        htmlFor="global-attention-threshold"
        style={{
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          justifyContent: 'space-between',
          fontSize: 11,
          color: 'rgb(148,163,184)',
          marginBottom: 6,
          userSelect: 'none',
        }}
      >
        <span style={{ color: 'rgb(96,165,250)', fontWeight: 600 }}>
          Attention threshold
        </span>
        <span style={{ fontFamily: 'monospace', color: 'rgb(203,213,225)' }}>
          {globalAttentionThreshold.toFixed(2)}
        </span>
      </label>
      <input
        id="global-attention-threshold"
        type="range"
        min={0}
        max={1}
        step={0.01}
        value={globalAttentionThreshold}
        onChange={(e) => setGlobalAttentionThreshold(Number(e.target.value))}
        style={{ width: '100%', accentColor: 'rgb(96,165,250)' }}
        aria-label="Global attention edge weight threshold"
      />
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import dynamic from 'next/dynamic';
import Link from 'next/link';

const MapVisualizer = dynamic(() => import('./MapVisualizer'), {
  ssr: false,
  loading: () => (
    <div className="flex h-screen w-screen items-center justify-center bg-neutral-950 text-slate-400 text-sm">
      Loading map…
    </div>
  ),
});

const TimeSlider = dynamic(() => import('./TimeSlider'), { ssr: false });
const NodeInspector = dynamic(() => import('./NodeInspector'), { ssr: false });
const AttentionControls = dynamic(() => import('./AttentionControls'), { ssr: false });

export default function MapScene() {
  return (
    <main className="relative h-screen w-screen overflow-hidden">
      <MapVisualizer />
      <NodeInspector />
      <TimeSlider />
      <AttentionControls />

      {/* Floating button: navigate to the Analytics dashboard.
          Positioned top-left to avoid conflicting with the NodeInspector (top-right)
          and the TimeSlider (bottom). */}
      <Link
        href="/analytics"
        aria-label="Open analytics dashboard"
        style={{
          position: 'absolute',
          top: 24,
          left: 24,
          zIndex: 20,
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          justifyContent: 'center',
          width: 48,
          height: 48,
          borderRadius: '50%',
          background: 'rgba(15,15,15,0.88)',
          border: '1px solid rgba(96,165,250,0.35)',
          backdropFilter: 'blur(8px)',
          color: '#60a5fa',
          textDecoration: 'none',
          boxShadow: '0 4px 24px rgba(0,0,0,0.5)',
          transition: 'border-color 0.15s, box-shadow 0.15s',
        }}
      >
        {/* Bar-chart icon (inline SVG — no MUI dep needed in this component) */}
        <svg width="22" height="22" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor" aria-hidden="true">
          <path d="M3 13h2v8H3v-8zm4-5h2v13H7V8zm4-4h2v17h-2V4zm4 7h2v10h-2V11zm4-4h2v14h-2V7z" />
        </svg>
      </Link>
    </main>
  );
}



================================================
FILE: frontend/src/components/MapVisualizer.tsx
================================================
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import maplibregl from "maplibre-gl";
import "maplibre-gl/dist/maplibre-gl.css";
import { MapboxOverlay } from "@deck.gl/mapbox";
import { ScatterplotLayer, ArcLayer } from "@deck.gl/layers";

import useTimeStore from "@/store/useTimeStore";
import type {
  UIPayload,
  NodeEntry,
  EdgeEntry,
  AttentionEdge,
} from "@/types/payload";

// Newcastle upon Tyne city centre
const INITIAL_VIEW = {
  longitude: -1.6178,
  latitude: 54.9783,
  zoom: 11,
  pitch: 45,
  bearing: 0,
};

const MAP_STYLE =
  "https://basemaps.cartocdn.com/gl/dark-matter-gl-style/style.json";

// Defensive cap on the global edge set when no node is selected.
const GLOBAL_EDGE_CAP = 80;

/**
 * Maps a normalised value t∈[0,1] to a Green→Yellow→Red RGBA tuple.
 */
function heatColor(t: number): [number, number, number, number] {
  const c = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, t));
  if (c < 0.5) {
    const s = c * 2;
    return [Math.round(s * 255), 200, 0, 220];
  }
  const s = (c - 0.5) * 2;
  return [255, Math.round(200 * (1 - s)), 0, 220];
}

function nodeFillColor(
  d: NodeEntry,
  timestep: number,
): [number, number, number, number] {
  const entry = d.timeseries[timestep];
  if (!entry) return [128, 128, 128, 180];
  const maxUncertainty = 50;
  const normalized = Math.min(1, entry.uncertainty / maxUncertainty);
  return heatColor(normalized);
}

function nodeRadius(d: NodeEntry, timestep: number): number {
  const entry = d.timeseries[timestep];
  const base = d.is_simulated ? 4 : 2;
  if (!entry) return base;
  return base + entry.prediction;
}

interface FilterState {
  selectedNodeId: number | null;
  attentionThreshold: number;
  hoveredNodeId: number | null;
  egoGraphEdges: AttentionEdge[] | null;
}

/**
 * Build all Deck.gl layers for the current frame.
 *
 * Layers (bottom to top):
 *   1. nodes-real       — solid ScatterplotLayer for physical sensors
 *   2. nodes-simulated  — hollow ring ScatterplotLayer for synthetic nodes
 *   3. topology-layer   — global or ego-graph ArcLayer (master_index edges)
 *   4. ego-graph-layer  — localized top-K ArcLayer from node_{id}.json
 */
function buildLayers(
  payload: UIPayload,
  nodeMap: Map<number, NodeEntry>,
  timestep: number,
  filters: FilterState,
  onSelectNode: (id: number | null) => void,
  onHoverNode: (id: number | null) => void,
) {
  const { selectedNodeId, attentionThreshold, hoveredNodeId, egoGraphEdges } =
    filters;

  // ── Real-node layer ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const realNodes = payload.nodes.filter((n) => !n.is_simulated);
  const realLayer = new ScatterplotLayer<NodeEntry>({
    id: "nodes-real",
    data: realNodes,
    getPosition: (d) => [d.lon, d.lat],
    getFillColor: (d) => nodeFillColor(d, timestep),
    getRadius: (d) =>
      nodeRadius(d, timestep) * (d.id === hoveredNodeId ? 1.6 : 1.0),
    radiusUnits: "meters",
    stroked: false,
    filled: true,
    pickable: true,
    autoHighlight: true,
    highlightColor: [255, 255, 255, 55],
    onClick: ({ object }) => {
      if (!object) return false;
      onSelectNode(object.id);
      return true;
    },
    onHover: ({ object }) => {
      onHoverNode(object ? object.id : null);
      return true;
    },
    updateTriggers: {
      getFillColor: [timestep],
      getRadius: [timestep, hoveredNodeId],
    },
  });

  // ── Simulated-node layer (hollow ring with transparent fill) ──────────────
  // filled: true + getFillColor: [0,0,0,0] creates an invisible solid disk that
  // covers the hollow centre, giving the full circle — not just the stroke — a
  // pickable hit-area. Without this the centre gap of the ring passes all
  // pointer events through to the map, making synthetic nodes unclickable.
  const simNodes = payload.nodes.filter((n) => n.is_simulated);
  const simLayer = new ScatterplotLayer<NodeEntry>({
    id: "nodes-simulated",
    data: simNodes,
    getPosition: (d) => [d.lon, d.lat],
    getLineColor: (d) => nodeFillColor(d, timestep),
    getFillColor: [0, 0, 0, 0],
    getRadius: (d) =>
      nodeRadius(d, timestep) * (d.id === hoveredNodeId ? 1.6 : 1.0),
    radiusUnits: "meters",
    stroked: true,
    filled: true,
    lineWidthMinPixels: 3,
    pickable: true,
    autoHighlight: true,
    highlightColor: [255, 255, 255, 55],
    onClick: ({ object }) => {
      if (!object) return false;
      onSelectNode(object.id);
      return true;
    },
    onHover: ({ object }) => {
      onHoverNode(object ? object.id : null);
      return true;
    },
    updateTriggers: {
      getLineColor: [timestep],
      getRadius: [timestep, hoveredNodeId],
    },
  });

  // ── Global topology layer (master_index edges) ─────────────────────────────
  type WeightedEdge = { edge: EdgeEntry; weight: number };
  let weighted: WeightedEdge[] = payload.edges
    .map((e) => ({ edge: e, weight: e.attention_sequence[timestep] ?? 0 }))
    .filter((x) => x.weight >= attentionThreshold);

  if (selectedNodeId !== null) {
    weighted = weighted.filter(
      (x) =>
        x.edge.source === selectedNodeId || x.edge.target === selectedNodeId,
    );
  } else {
    weighted = weighted
      .sort((a, b) => b.weight - a.weight)
      .slice(0, GLOBAL_EDGE_CAP);
  }

  const arcLayer = new ArcLayer<WeightedEdge>({
    id: "topology-layer",
    data: weighted,
    visible: selectedNodeId === null,
    getSourcePosition: (d) => {
      const src = nodeMap.get(d.edge.source);
      return src ? [src.lon, src.lat] : [0, 0];
    },
    getTargetPosition: (d) => {
      const tgt = nodeMap.get(d.edge.target);
      return tgt ? [tgt.lon, tgt.lat] : [0, 0];
    },
    getSourceColor: (d) => [255, 165, 0, Math.round(10 + d.weight * 245)],
    getTargetColor: (d) => [255, 69, 0, Math.round(10 + d.weight * 245)],
    getWidth: (d) => Math.max(1, d.weight * 80),
    updateTriggers: {
      getSourceColor: [timestep],
      getTargetColor: [timestep],
      getWidth: [timestep],
      data: [timestep, selectedNodeId, attentionThreshold],
    },
  });

  // ── Localized ego-graph layer (top-K edges from node_{id}.json) ────────────
  // Blue arcs = incoming attention; rose arcs = outgoing attention.
  // Width and alpha scale with weight_sequence[currentTimestep]. Visible only when a
  // node is selected and ego-graph data has been fetched by NodeInspector.
  const currentTimestep = timestep;
  const egoArcLayer = new ArcLayer<AttentionEdge>({
    id: "ego-graph-layer",
    data: egoGraphEdges ?? [],
    visible: selectedNodeId !== null && (egoGraphEdges?.length ?? 0) > 0,
    getSourcePosition: (d) => [d.source_lon, d.source_lat],
    getTargetPosition: (d) => [d.target_lon, d.target_lat],
    getSourceColor: (d) => {
      const w = d.weight_sequence[currentTimestep] ?? 0;
      const alpha = Math.round(80 + w * 175);
      return d.direction === "incoming"
        ? [96, 165, 250, alpha]
        : [251, 113, 133, alpha];
    },
    getTargetColor: (d) => {
      const w = d.weight_sequence[currentTimestep] ?? 0;
      const alpha = Math.round(50 + w * 110);
      return d.direction === "incoming"
        ? [96, 165, 250, alpha]
        : [251, 113, 133, alpha];
    },
    getWidth: (d) => {
      const w = d.weight_sequence[currentTimestep] ?? 0;
      // Tripwire: must fire continuously while scrubbing TimeSlider. If this
      // logs only once at mount, the layer's updateTriggers are not wired and
      // accessors are closing over a stale currentTimestep.
      console.log(
        `[ego-arc] getWidth t=${currentTimestep} edge=${d.source_id}->${d.target_id} w=${w.toFixed(3)}`,
      );
      return w * 200;
    },
    widthUnits: "pixels",
    updateTriggers: {
      getWidth: [currentTimestep],
      getSourceColor: [currentTimestep],
      getTargetColor: [currentTimestep],
      // Re-evaluate the data binding when timestep advances or the underlying
      // edges array changes (e.g. a different node was selected).
      data: [currentTimestep, egoGraphEdges],
    },
  });

  return [realLayer, simLayer, arcLayer, egoArcLayer];
}

export default function MapVisualizer() {
  const mapContainerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const mapRef = useRef<maplibregl.Map | null>(null);
  const overlayRef = useRef<MapboxOverlay | null>(null);

  const payloadRef = useRef<UIPayload | null>(null);
  const nodeMapRef = useRef<Map<number, NodeEntry>>(new Map());

  // Hover state is kept in a ref (not Zustand) to avoid React re-renders on
  // every pointer movement — only the WebGL overlay needs to rebuild.
  const hoveredNodeIdRef = useRef<number | null>(null);

  // Self-referential hover rebuild function. Set once after data loads, updated
  // on every state-effect run. Always reads live values from useTimeStore.getState()
  // and from stable refs, so the closure never goes stale.
  //
  // The indirection through a ref (rather than a plain closure) is what breaks
  // the "dead callback" bug: every buildLayers call passes
  //   onHoverNode: (id) => rebuildHoverRef.current(id)
  // so the next hover invocation always calls the *current* rebuild function,
  // not a snapshot of it from the previous render.
  const rebuildHoverRef = useRef<(id: number | null) => void>(() => {});

  const setMaxTimesteps = useTimeStore((s) => s.setMaxTimesteps);
  const setSelectedNodeId = useTimeStore((s) => s.setSelectedNodeId);
  const currentTimestep = useTimeStore((s) => s.currentTimestep);
  const selectedNodeId = useTimeStore((s) => s.selectedNodeId);
  const globalAttentionThreshold = useTimeStore(
    (s) => s.globalAttentionThreshold,
  );
  const egoGraphEdges = useTimeStore((s) => s.egoGraphEdges);

  // ── Initialise MapLibre + deck.gl overlay once ────────────────────────────
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mapContainerRef.current || mapRef.current) return;

    const map = new maplibregl.Map({
      container: mapContainerRef.current,
      style: MAP_STYLE,
      center: [INITIAL_VIEW.longitude, INITIAL_VIEW.latitude],
      zoom: INITIAL_VIEW.zoom,
      pitch: INITIAL_VIEW.pitch,
      bearing: INITIAL_VIEW.bearing,
    });

    map.addControl(new maplibregl.NavigationControl(), "top-right");

    const overlay = new MapboxOverlay({ layers: [] });
    map.addControl(overlay as unknown as maplibregl.IControl);

    mapRef.current = map;
    overlayRef.current = overlay;

    // Only deselect on a bare map click (no node under the cursor).
    // When the cursor is on a node, hoveredNodeIdRef is non-null and the
    // layer's own onClick handler is responsible for setting the selection.
    // Without this guard the map click fires AFTER the layer click and
    // immediately nulls out the selection the layer just made.
    map.on("click", () => {
      if (hoveredNodeIdRef.current !== null) return;
      if (useTimeStore.getState().selectedNodeId !== null) {
        useTimeStore.getState().setSelectedNodeId(null);
      }
    });

    map.on("load", async () => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch("/data/master_index.json");
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
        const payload: UIPayload = await res.json();

        setMaxTimesteps(payload.metadata.timesteps - 1);

        const nodeMap = new Map<number, NodeEntry>();
        for (const node of payload.nodes) nodeMap.set(node.id, node);

        payloadRef.current = payload;
        nodeMapRef.current = nodeMap;

        const simNodes = payload.nodes.filter((n) => n.is_simulated);
        console.info(
          `[MapVisualizer] Payload loaded: ${payload.nodes.length} nodes` +
            ` (${payload.metadata.num_real_nodes} real + ${simNodes.length} simulated),` +
            ` ${payload.edges.length} edges, ${payload.metadata.timesteps} timesteps.`,
        );

        // Initialise the self-referential hover rebuild function.
        // Uses payloadRef/nodeMapRef/overlayRef so it stays correct if refs update.
        rebuildHoverRef.current = (id: number | null) => {
          if (hoveredNodeIdRef.current === id) return;
          hoveredNodeIdRef.current = id;
          const ov = overlayRef.current;
          if (!ov) return;
          const s = useTimeStore.getState();
          ov.setProps({
            layers: buildLayers(
              payloadRef.current!,
              nodeMapRef.current,
              s.currentTimestep,
              {
                selectedNodeId: s.selectedNodeId,
                attentionThreshold: s.globalAttentionThreshold,
                hoveredNodeId: id,
                egoGraphEdges: s.egoGraphEdges,
              },
              setSelectedNodeId,
              (id2) => rebuildHoverRef.current(id2),
            ),
          });
        };

        const state = useTimeStore.getState();
        overlay.setProps({
          layers: buildLayers(
            payload,
            nodeMap,
            state.currentTimestep,
            {
              selectedNodeId: state.selectedNodeId,
              attentionThreshold: state.globalAttentionThreshold,
              hoveredNodeId: hoveredNodeIdRef.current,
              egoGraphEdges: state.egoGraphEdges,
            },
            setSelectedNodeId,
            (id) => rebuildHoverRef.current(id),
          ),
        });
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn("[MapVisualizer] Failed to load master_index.json", err);
      }
    });

    return () => {
      overlay.finalize();
      map.remove();
      mapRef.current = null;
      overlayRef.current = null;
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  // Rebuild layers whenever timestep, selection, threshold, or ego-graph data changes.
  // Also refreshes rebuildHoverRef.current so hover callbacks always see the latest
  // Zustand snapshot (selectedNodeId, attentionThreshold, egoGraphEdges).
  useEffect(() => {
    const payload = payloadRef.current;
    const overlay = overlayRef.current;
    if (!payload || !overlay) return;

    // Keep the hover rebuild function in sync with the latest closed-over state.
    rebuildHoverRef.current = (id: number | null) => {
      if (hoveredNodeIdRef.current === id) return;
      hoveredNodeIdRef.current = id;
      const ov = overlayRef.current;
      if (!ov) return;
      const s = useTimeStore.getState();
      ov.setProps({
        layers: buildLayers(
          payloadRef.current!,
          nodeMapRef.current,
          s.currentTimestep,
          {
            selectedNodeId: s.selectedNodeId,
            attentionThreshold: s.globalAttentionThreshold,
            hoveredNodeId: id,
            egoGraphEdges: s.egoGraphEdges,
          },
          setSelectedNodeId,
          (id2) => rebuildHoverRef.current(id2),
        ),
      });
    };

    overlay.setProps({
      layers: buildLayers(
        payload,
        nodeMapRef.current,
        currentTimestep,
        {
          selectedNodeId,
          attentionThreshold: globalAttentionThreshold,
          hoveredNodeId: hoveredNodeIdRef.current,
          egoGraphEdges,
        },
        setSelectedNodeId,
        (id) => rebuildHoverRef.current(id),
      ),
    });
  }, [
    currentTimestep,
    selectedNodeId,
    globalAttentionThreshold,
    egoGraphEdges,
    setSelectedNodeId,
  ]);

  return (
    <div
      ref={mapContainerRef}
      style={{ width: "100%", height: "100%" }}
      aria-label="Geospatial digital twin map"
    />
  );
}



================================================
FILE: frontend/src/components/NodeInspector.tsx
================================================
"use client";

import { useEffect, useMemo } from "react";
import useTimeStore from "@/store/useTimeStore";
import type { NodeDetailPayload, AttentionEdge } from "@/types/payload";
import { useState } from "react";

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// SVG layout constants (shared by both charts)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

const PLOT_W = 320;
const PAD = { top: 8, right: 8, bottom: 18, left: 30 };
const INNER_W = PLOT_W - PAD.left - PAD.right;

// Imputation chart height
const IMPUTE_H = 140;
const IMPUTE_INNER_H = IMPUTE_H - PAD.top - PAD.bottom;

// Pi MNAR timeline height (shorter — pi is bounded [0,1])
const PI_H = 90;
const PI_INNER_H = PI_H - PAD.top - PAD.bottom;

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Imputation chart (existing — unchanged)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

interface ImpChartProps {
  node: NodeDetailPayload;
  currentTimestep: number;
}

function ImpuationChart({ node, currentTimestep }: ImpChartProps) {
  const T = node.imputed_series.length;

  const { yMin, yMax } = useMemo(() => {
    const vals: number[] = [];
    for (const v of node.ground_truth_series) if (v !== null) vals.push(v);
    for (const v of node.imputed_series) vals.push(v);
    if (vals.length === 0) return { yMin: 0, yMax: 1 };
    const lo = Math.min(...vals);
    const hi = Math.max(...vals);
    const span = Math.max(1, hi - lo) * 0.05;
    return { yMin: Math.max(0, lo - span), yMax: hi + span };
  }, [node]);

  const xOf = (t: number) => PAD.left + (T <= 1 ? 0 : (t / (T - 1)) * INNER_W);
  const yOf = (v: number) =>
    PAD.top +
    IMPUTE_INNER_H -
    ((v - yMin) / (yMax - yMin || 1)) * IMPUTE_INNER_H;

  const gtSegments = useMemo(() => {
    const segs: string[] = [];
    let cur: string[] = [];
    for (let t = 0; t < T; t++) {
      const v = node.ground_truth_series[t];
      if (v === null || v === undefined) {
        if (cur.length) segs.push(cur.join(" "));
        cur = [];
      } else {
        cur.push(`${xOf(t)},${yOf(v)}`);
      }
    }
    if (cur.length) segs.push(cur.join(" "));
    return segs;
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [node, yMin, yMax]);

  const imputedPoints = useMemo(() => {
    return node.imputed_series.map((v, t) => `${xOf(t)},${yOf(v)}`).join(" ");
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [node, yMin, yMax]);

  const maskBands = useMemo(() => {
    const bandW = T <= 1 ? INNER_W : INNER_W / (T - 1);
    return node.mask_series
      .map((m, t) => (m ? t : -1))
      .filter((t) => t >= 0)
      .map((t) => ({ x: xOf(t) - bandW / 2, w: bandW }));
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [node]);

  const cursorX = xOf(currentTimestep);

  return (
    <svg
      width={PLOT_W}
      height={IMPUTE_H}
      viewBox={`0 0 ${PLOT_W} ${IMPUTE_H}`}
      role="img"
      aria-label={`Imputation chart for ${node.location_name}`}
    >
      {maskBands.map((b, i) => (
        <rect
          key={i}
          x={b.x}
          y={PAD.top}
          width={b.w}
          height={IMPUTE_INNER_H}
          fill="rgba(255, 99, 132, 0.18)"
        />
      ))}
      <rect
        x={PAD.left}
        y={PAD.top}
        width={INNER_W}
        height={IMPUTE_INNER_H}
        fill="none"
        stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.2)"
      />
      <text
        x={PAD.left - 4}
        y={PAD.top + 4}
        fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.6)"
        fontSize="9"
        textAnchor="end"
      >
        {yMax.toFixed(1)}
      </text>
      <text
        x={PAD.left - 4}
        y={PAD.top + IMPUTE_INNER_H}
        fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.6)"
        fontSize="9"
        textAnchor="end"
      >
        {yMin.toFixed(1)}
      </text>
      <text
        x={PAD.left}
        y={IMPUTE_H - 4}
        fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.6)"
        fontSize="9"
      >
        t=0
      </text>
      <text
        x={PAD.left + INNER_W}
        y={IMPUTE_H - 4}
        fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.6)"
        fontSize="9"
        textAnchor="end"
      >
        t={T - 1}
      </text>
      <polyline
        points={imputedPoints}
        fill="none"
        stroke="rgb(255, 165, 0)"
        strokeWidth="1.5"
      />
      {gtSegments.map((seg, i) => (
        <polyline
          key={i}
          points={seg}
          fill="none"
          stroke="rgb(96, 165, 250)"
          strokeWidth="1.5"
        />
      ))}
      <line
        x1={cursorX}
        y1={PAD.top}
        x2={cursorX}
        y2={PAD.top + IMPUTE_INNER_H}
        stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.55)"
        strokeWidth="1"
        strokeDasharray="2 2"
      />
    </svg>
  );
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Pi MNAR Ghosting Timeline (custom SVG — no external charting dependency)
//
// Renders π (zero-inflation probability) over time as a violet polyline.
// Timesteps where is_missing === true are shaded red before the line is drawn
// (same visual idiom as the imputation chart's mask bands).
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

interface PiChartProps {
  node: NodeDetailPayload;
  currentTimestep: number;
}

function PiChart({ node, currentTimestep }: PiChartProps) {
  const series = node.zinb_series;
  const T = series.length;

  // Guard: old payloads without pi field degrade gracefully
  if (T === 0 || series[0].pi === undefined) return null;

  const xOf = (t: number) => PAD.left + (T <= 1 ? 0 : (t / (T - 1)) * INNER_W);
  // pi is always in (0,1) — fixed Y axis
  const yOf = (v: number) => PAD.top + PI_INNER_H - v * PI_INNER_H;

  // Contiguous runs of is_missing === true for background shading
  const missingRuns = useMemo(() => {
    const runs: { x1: number; x2: number }[] = [];
    let start: number | null = null;
    for (const { t, is_missing } of series) {
      if (is_missing && start === null) start = t;
      if (!is_missing && start !== null) {
        runs.push({ x1: xOf(start), x2: xOf(t - 1) });
        start = null;
      }
    }
    if (start !== null) runs.push({ x1: xOf(start), x2: xOf(T - 1) });
    return runs;
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [series]);

  const piPoints = series.map(({ t, pi }) => `${xOf(t)},${yOf(pi)}`).join(" ");
  const cursorX = xOf(currentTimestep);
  const currentPi = series[currentTimestep]?.pi;

  return (
    <svg
      width={PLOT_W}
      height={PI_H}
      viewBox={`0 0 ${PLOT_W} ${PI_H}`}
      role="img"
      aria-label={`Pi MNAR timeline for ${node.location_name}`}
    >
      {/* Red shading for masked timesteps (rendered first — behind the line) */}
      {missingRuns.map((r, i) => (
        <rect
          key={i}
          x={r.x1}
          y={PAD.top}
          width={Math.max(1, r.x2 - r.x1)}
          height={PI_INNER_H}
          fill="rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.22)"
        />
      ))}
      {/* Chart border */}
      <rect
        x={PAD.left}
        y={PAD.top}
        width={INNER_W}
        height={PI_INNER_H}
        fill="none"
        stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.2)"
      />
      {/* Y-axis labels: 0 and 1 */}
      <text
        x={PAD.left - 4}
        y={PAD.top + 4}
        fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.5)"
        fontSize="9"
        textAnchor="end"
      >
        1
      </text>
      <text
        x={PAD.left - 4}
        y={PAD.top + PI_INNER_H}
        fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.5)"
        fontSize="9"
        textAnchor="end"
      >
        0
      </text>
      {/* 0.5 reference line */}
      <line
        x1={PAD.left}
        y1={PAD.top + PI_INNER_H / 2}
        x2={PAD.left + INNER_W}
        y2={PAD.top + PI_INNER_H / 2}
        stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.08)"
        strokeWidth="1"
        strokeDasharray="3 3"
      />
      {/* X-axis labels */}
      <text x={PAD.left} y={PI_H - 4} fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.5)" fontSize="9">
        t=0
      </text>
      <text
        x={PAD.left + INNER_W}
        y={PI_H - 4}
        fill="rgba(255,255,255,0.5)"
        fontSize="9"
        textAnchor="end"
      >
        t={T - 1}
      </text>
      {/* Pi polyline */}
      <polyline
        points={piPoints}
        fill="none"
        stroke="rgb(167, 139, 250)"
        strokeWidth="1.5"
      />
      {/* Current timestep cursor */}
      <line
        x1={cursorX}
        y1={PAD.top}
        x2={cursorX}
        y2={PAD.top + PI_INNER_H}
        stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.55)"
        strokeWidth="1"
        strokeDasharray="2 2"
      />
      {/* Pi value at cursor */}
      {currentPi !== undefined && (
        <text
          x={Math.min(cursorX + 3, PAD.left + INNER_W - 22)}
          y={PAD.top + 9}
          fill="rgb(167,139,250)"
          fontSize="8"
        >
          {currentPi.toFixed(3)}
        </text>
      )}
    </svg>
  );
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Attention Edge rank list
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function EdgeList({ edges }: { edges: AttentionEdge[] }) {
  const currentTimestep = useTimeStore((s) => s.currentTimestep);

  const { topIncoming, topOutgoing } = useMemo(() => {
    const byWeight = (a: AttentionEdge, b: AttentionEdge) =>
      (b.weight_sequence[currentTimestep] ?? 0) -
      (a.weight_sequence[currentTimestep] ?? 0);
    return {
      topIncoming: edges
        .filter((e) => e.direction === "incoming")
        .sort(byWeight)
        .slice(0, 5),
      topOutgoing: edges
        .filter((e) => e.direction === "outgoing")
        .sort(byWeight)
        .slice(0, 5),
    };
  }, [edges, currentTimestep]);

  const sorted = [...topIncoming, ...topOutgoing];
  const maxW = Math.max(
    ...sorted.map((e) => e.weight_sequence[currentTimestep] ?? 0),
    0.001,
  );

  return (
    <ul className="mt-1 space-y-1">
      {sorted.map((e, i) => {
        const isIncoming = e.direction === "incoming";
        const neighborId = isIncoming ? e.source_id : e.target_id;
        const w = e.weight_sequence[currentTimestep] ?? 0;
        const barW = Math.round((w / maxW) * 80);
        return (
          <li key={i} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px]">
            {/* Direction arrow */}
            <span
              className={`w-3 text-center font-bold ${isIncoming ? "text-blue-400" : "text-amber-400"}`}
              title={isIncoming ? "Incoming" : "Outgoing"}
            >
              {isIncoming ? "←" : "→"}
            </span>
            {/* Neighbor ID */}
            <span className="w-14 font-mono text-slate-300 truncate">
              Node {neighborId}
            </span>
            {/* Weight bar */}
            <span
              className="inline-block rounded-sm"
              style={{
                width: barW,
                minWidth: 2,
                height: 3,
                background: isIncoming
                  ? "rgb(96, 165, 250)"
                  : "rgb(251, 146, 60)",
              }}
            />
            {/* Numeric weight */}
            <span className="ml-auto font-mono text-slate-400">
              {w.toFixed(3)}
            </span>
          </li>
        );
      })}
    </ul>
  );
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Main NodeInspector component
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

export default function NodeInspector() {
  const selectedNodeId = useTimeStore((s) => s.selectedNodeId);
  const setSelectedNodeId = useTimeStore((s) => s.setSelectedNodeId);
  const currentTimestep = useTimeStore((s) => s.currentTimestep);
  const setEgoGraphEdges = useTimeStore((s) => s.setEgoGraphEdges);

  const [nodeDetail, setNodeDetail] = useState<NodeDetailPayload | null>(null);

  // Lazy per-node fetch: load node_{id}.json whenever the selected node changes.
  // Write ego_graph_edges into the store so MapVisualizer can render the ArcLayer.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedNodeId === null) {
      setNodeDetail(null);
      setEgoGraphEdges(null);
      return;
    }
    fetch(`/data/nodes/node_${selectedNodeId}.json`)
      .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : null))
      .then((d: NodeDetailPayload | null) => {
        setNodeDetail(d);
        setEgoGraphEdges(d?.ego_graph_edges ?? null);
      })
      .catch(() => {
        setNodeDetail(null);
        setEgoGraphEdges(null);
      });
  }, [selectedNodeId, setEgoGraphEdges]);

  if (selectedNodeId === null) return null;
  if (!nodeDetail) return null;

  const hasImputation =
    Array.isArray(nodeDetail.imputed_series) &&
    Array.isArray(nodeDetail.ground_truth_series) &&
    Array.isArray(nodeDetail.mask_series) &&
    nodeDetail.imputed_series.length > 0;

  const hasPi =
    Array.isArray(nodeDetail.zinb_series) &&
    nodeDetail.zinb_series.length > 0 &&
    nodeDetail.zinb_series[0].pi !== undefined;

  const hasEgoEdges =
    Array.isArray(nodeDetail.ego_graph_edges) &&
    nodeDetail.ego_graph_edges.length > 0;

  const currentZINB = nodeDetail.zinb_series?.[currentTimestep];
  const currentMasked = nodeDetail.mask_series?.[currentTimestep] ?? false;
  const currentImputed = nodeDetail.imputed_series?.[currentTimestep];
  const currentGT = nodeDetail.ground_truth_series?.[currentTimestep];

  return (
    <aside
      style={{
        position: "absolute",
        top: 16,
        right: 16,
        width: 360,
        zIndex: 10,
        maxHeight: "calc(100vh - 32px)",
      }}
      className="rounded-lg border border-white/10 bg-neutral-950/85 p-4 text-xs text-slate-200 shadow-xl backdrop-blur overflow-y-auto"
    >
      {/* Header */}
      <header className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <div className="truncate font-semibold text-sm">
            {nodeDetail.location_name}
          </div>
          <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">
            id={nodeDetail.id} · lat={nodeDetail.lat.toFixed(4)} · lon=
            {nodeDetail.lon.toFixed(4)}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          {nodeDetail.is_simulated && (
            <span className="rounded bg-amber-600/20 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium text-amber-300">
              SIMULATED
            </span>
          )}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setSelectedNodeId(null)}
            className="rounded px-2 py-0.5 text-slate-300 hover:bg-white/10"
            aria-label="Close inspector"
          >
            ✕
          </button>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* ── Imputation chart ── */}
      {hasImputation ? (
        <>
          <div className="mt-3">
            <ImpuationChart
              node={nodeDetail}
              currentTimestep={currentTimestep}
            />
          </div>
          <div className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-3 text-[10px] text-slate-400">
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
              <span className="inline-block h-0.5 w-3 bg-blue-400" /> Ground
              truth
            </span>
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
              <span className="inline-block h-0.5 w-3 bg-orange-400" /> Imputed
            </span>
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
              <span className="inline-block h-2 w-3 bg-rose-400/30" /> Masked
            </span>
          </div>
          <dl className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-2 gap-1 text-[11px]">
            <dt className="text-slate-400">t = {currentTimestep}</dt>
            <dd className="text-right">
              {currentMasked ? "masked" : "observed"}
            </dd>
            <dt className="text-slate-400">ground truth</dt>
            <dd className="text-right">
              {currentGT === null || currentGT === undefined
                ? "—"
                : currentGT.toFixed(2)}
            </dd>
            <dt className="text-slate-400">imputed</dt>
            <dd className="text-right">
              {currentImputed === undefined ? "—" : currentImputed.toFixed(2)}
            </dd>
            {currentZINB && (
              <>
                <dt className="text-slate-400">µ (forecast)</dt>
                <dd className="text-right">{currentZINB.mu.toFixed(2)}</dd>
                <dt className="text-slate-400">θ (dispersion)</dt>
                <dd className="text-right">{currentZINB.theta.toFixed(2)}</dd>
                <dt className="text-slate-400">σ (uncertainty)</dt>
                <dd className="text-right">{currentZINB.std_dev.toFixed(2)}</dd>
                {currentZINB.pi !== undefined && (
                  <>
                    <dt className="text-slate-400">π (zero-inflation)</dt>
                    <dd className="text-right text-violet-300">
                      {currentZINB.pi.toFixed(3)}
                    </dd>
                  </>
                )}
              </>
            )}
          </dl>
        </>
      ) : (
        <div className="mt-3 text-slate-400">
          Imputation data unavailable in this payload.
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ── MNAR Ghosting Timeline (π over time) ── */}
      {hasPi && (
        <div className="mt-4 border-t border-white/10 pt-3">
          <div className="mb-1 flex items-center justify-between text-[10px] text-slate-400">
            <span>
              <span className="font-medium text-violet-300">π</span>{" "}
              zero-inflation probability
            </span>
            <span className="text-rose-400/70">red = masked</span>
          </div>
          <PiChart node={nodeDetail} currentTimestep={currentTimestep} />
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ── Top Attention Edges (Ego-Graph) ── */}
      {hasEgoEdges && (
        <div className="mt-4 border-t border-white/10 pt-3">
          <div className="mb-1 flex items-center justify-between text-[10px]">
            <span className="font-medium text-slate-300">
              Top Attention Edges
            </span>
            <span className="text-slate-500">
              <span className="text-blue-400">←</span> in{" "}
              <span className="text-amber-400">→</span> out
            </span>
          </div>
          <EdgeList edges={nodeDetail.ego_graph_edges!} />
        </div>
      )}
    </aside>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';
import useTimeStore from '@/store/useTimeStore';

// Each timestep represents 15 minutes; anchor to midnight of the test day.
function timestepToLabel(t: number): string {
  const totalMinutes = t * 15;
  const hh = Math.floor(totalMinutes / 60)
    .toString()
    .padStart(2, '0');
  const mm = (totalMinutes % 60).toString().padStart(2, '0');
  return `${hh}:${mm}`;
}

export default function TimeSlider() {
  const currentTimestep = useTimeStore((s) => s.currentTimestep);
  const maxTimesteps = useTimeStore((s) => s.maxTimesteps);
  const isPlaying = useTimeStore((s) => s.isPlaying);
  const setTimestep = useTimeStore((s) => s.setTimestep);
  const togglePlayback = useTimeStore((s) => s.togglePlayback);
  const incrementTimestep = useTimeStore((s) => s.incrementTimestep);

  // Log every timestep change so the test can be verified in the console
  useEffect(() => {
    console.log(`[TimeSlider] currentTimestep=${currentTimestep}  (${timestepToLabel(currentTimestep)})`);
  }, [currentTimestep]);

  // Playback animation loop
  const rafRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const lastTickRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const TICK_INTERVAL_MS = 300; // one step every 300 ms

  const animate = useCallback(
    (now: number) => {
      if (now - lastTickRef.current >= TICK_INTERVAL_MS) {
        incrementTimestep();
        lastTickRef.current = now;
      }
      rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    },
    [incrementTimestep]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isPlaying) {
      lastTickRef.current = performance.now();
      rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    } else {
      if (rafRef.current !== null) {
        cancelAnimationFrame(rafRef.current);
        rafRef.current = null;
      }
    }
    return () => {
      if (rafRef.current !== null) {
        cancelAnimationFrame(rafRef.current);
      }
    };
  }, [isPlaying, animate]);

  return (
    <div
      className="
        absolute bottom-6 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2
        flex flex-col items-center gap-2
        bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-sm text-white
        rounded-xl px-6 py-4 shadow-2xl
        w-[min(560px,90vw)]
        z-10
      "
      aria-label="Time navigation controls"
    >
      {/* Header row */}
      <div className="flex w-full items-center justify-between text-xs font-mono text-slate-300">
        <span>2024-05-10</span>
        <span className="text-base font-semibold text-white tabular-nums">
          {timestepToLabel(currentTimestep)}
        </span>
        <span>
          t = {currentTimestep} / {maxTimesteps}
        </span>
      </div>

      {/* Slider */}
      <input
        type="range"
        min={0}
        max={maxTimesteps}
        value={currentTimestep}
        onChange={(e) => setTimestep(Number(e.target.value))}
        className="w-full h-2 appearance-none rounded-full cursor-pointer accent-orange-400"
        aria-label="Timestep selector"
      />

      {/* Tick marks for every 4 steps (= 1 hour) */}
      <div className="flex w-full justify-between px-0.5">
        {Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, i) => (
          <span key={i} className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-mono">
            {String(i * 4).padStart(2, '0')}h
          </span>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Play / Pause */}
      <button
        onClick={togglePlayback}
        className="
          mt-1 px-5 py-1.5 rounded-full text-sm font-medium
          bg-orange-500 hover:bg-orange-400 active:bg-orange-600
          transition-colors focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-orange-300
        "
        aria-label={isPlaying ? 'Pause playback' : 'Play time animation'}
      >
        {isPlaying ? '⏸ Pause' : '▶ Play'}
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}



================================================
FILE: frontend/src/store/useTimeStore.ts
================================================
import { create } from 'zustand';
import type { AttentionEdge } from '@/types/payload';

interface TimeState {
  currentTimestep: number;
  maxTimesteps: number;
  isPlaying: boolean;
  selectedNodeId: number | null;
  globalAttentionThreshold: number;
  egoGraphEdges: AttentionEdge[] | null;

  setTimestep: (step: number) => void;
  setMaxTimesteps: (max: number) => void;
  togglePlayback: () => void;
  incrementTimestep: () => void;
  setSelectedNodeId: (id: number | null) => void;
  setGlobalAttentionThreshold: (v: number) => void;
  setEgoGraphEdges: (edges: AttentionEdge[] | null) => void;
}

const useTimeStore = create<TimeState>((set, get) => ({
  currentTimestep: 0,
  maxTimesteps: 95,
  isPlaying: false,
  selectedNodeId: null,
  globalAttentionThreshold: 0.5,
  egoGraphEdges: null,

  setTimestep: (step: number) =>
    set({ currentTimestep: Math.max(0, Math.min(step, get().maxTimesteps)) }),

  setMaxTimesteps: (max: number) => set({ maxTimesteps: max }),

  togglePlayback: () => set((state) => ({ isPlaying: !state.isPlaying })),

  incrementTimestep: () =>
    set((state) => ({
      currentTimestep:
        state.currentTimestep >= state.maxTimesteps
          ? 0
          : state.currentTimestep + 1,
    })),

  setSelectedNodeId: (id: number | null) => set({ selectedNodeId: id }),

  // Clamp threshold to [0, 1] — attention weights live in [0, 1].
  setGlobalAttentionThreshold: (v: number) =>
    set({ globalAttentionThreshold: Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v)) }),

  setEgoGraphEdges: (edges: AttentionEdge[] | null) => set({ egoGraphEdges: edges }),
}));

export default useTimeStore;
